State { I want to be the very best ... After this, we will **finally be ready to build our first Agent!** Great question! Data refers to raw facts, statistics, or numbers that have not been analyzed or processed into any meaningful context. On the other hand, information is data that has been organized, analyzed, and interpreted in a way that it can be used by humans to make informed decisions or take actions. In other words, data is raw material, while information is processed and useful knowledge.
